# scripts/wpt_baseline.txt -- SHRINK-ONLY record of what the native
# engine does NOT pass in the vendored WPT subset (tests/wpt/).
#
# The ratchet (scripts/test_wpt_ratchet_host.sh) fails when a subtest
# that was PASSING starts failing, or when the total PASS count drops
# below #!PASS_FLOOR. Failures DISAPPEARING is the point of the
# exercise: fix engine bugs, then regenerate with
#   bash scripts/test_wpt_ratchet_host.sh --regen
#
# Two kinds of row, both TAB-separated <test path>\t<subtest name>:
#   <path>\t<subtest>     -- did NOT pass at baseline
#   +<path>\t<subtest>    -- DID pass at baseline (the regression set)
#
# Recording the passes is what makes a regression decidable. A newly
# appearing failure row is ambiguous by itself: the subtest may have
# regressed, or it may never have reported before. Only a subtest that
# was in the pass set and no longer is counts as a regression --
# and #!PASS_FLOOR catches any genuine loss a second time regardless.
#
# A line reading '<FILE PRODUCED NO RESULTS>' means the whole file produced
# no harness output at all.
#!PASS_FLOOR 2032
